   n=len(h); dx=2/n; total=n/2
   # prefix via np.cumsum
   p=np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(h)))

delta_pos/dx = delta*(right_val - prod)
delta_neg/dx = delta*(left_val - prod)
W[d_pos] += delta_pos; W[d_neg] += delta_neg.

Recompute W exact:
```
def recompute_W(h):
 n=len(h); dx=2/n
 h2h = np.dot(h,h)
 S = np.sum(h)
 V0 = dx*(S - h2h)
 prefix = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(h)))
 # aut pos d via correlate full center:
 aut_full = np.correlate(h,h, mode='full')
 aut = aut_full[n-1:n-1+n]  # length n: aut[0]=sum squares, aut[d]=sum h_i h_{i+d}
 d = np.arange(1,n)
 pos = dx*(prefix[n-d] - aut[1:])
 neg = dx*(S - prefix[d] - aut[1:])
 W=np.empty(2*n-1)
 W[0]=V0
 W[1::2]=pos
 W[2::2]=neg
 return W
```
Check neg prefix[d] sum first d, yes S-prefix[d] sum from d to n-1.
Could use total S not target for recompute. For exact objective, if h not exactly sum target, but we'll maintain target. The evaluator expects sum exactly; use target for accept? We'll project.

For total pair transfer, if we call `apply_delta` for i then j, the second uses updated h_i as product neighbor if distance d=|i-j|. That is correct as sequential. If reject, reverse using current values.
But `apply_delta` should not need to store old because it uses current h[p] as base. It updates h[p]=h[p]+delta. Formula for W0 uses old base? If h[p] current a before change. For other W: new = old + dx*delta*(linearity - (right_old? neighbor value before p change) ...). For product with neighbor q, if q changed earlier in same move, current neighbor value is new; that's correct sequential. But formula uses current h[p] as a before this single delta; okay.

Implementation of `apply_delta(W,h,p,delta, dx, n)` without allocation:
```
a = h[p]
W[0] += dx * (delta * (1.0 - 2.0*a - delta))
h[p] = a + delta
# right side: d=1..n-1-p, neighbor index p+d
right_count = n - 1 - p
left_count = p
# For d up to min, we need combine right and left neighbors.
# We can loop d=1..n-1:
for d in range(1,n):
   linpos = 1.0 if d <= right_count else 0.0
   linneg = 1.0 if d <= left_count else 0.0
   prod = 0.0
   if d <= right_count: prod += h[p+d]
   if d <= left_count: prod += h[p-d]
   W[2*d-1] += dx*delta*(linpos - prod)
   W[2*d] += dx*delta*(linneg - prod)
```
But for d where neither right nor left? Impossible? If p interior, for d max one side true; if p=0 right true for d≤n-1, left false; if p=n-1 left true. Good.
We can avoid if per d by splitting loops:
- d = 1..min(right_count, left_count): both
- then only right or only left.
Both sides:
```
for d in range(1, both+1):
   prod = h[p+d] + h[p-d]
   W[2*d-1] += c*(1 - prod)
   W[2*d] += c*(1 - prod)  # both linpos and linneg =1
```
Wait if both true, linpos=linneg=1. Yes.
Only right (if right_count>left_count): d=left_count+1..right_count:
```
prod = h[p+d]; Wpos += c*(1 - prod); Wneg += c*(0 - prod) = -c*prod
```
Only left: d=right_count+1..left_count:
```
prod = h[p-d]; Wpos += -c*prod; Wneg += c*(1 - prod)
```
This reduces branches. For p, if both=0? If p at edges, only one side.
Need be careful h[p+d] access after h[p] updated? h[p] not neighbor for any d, so fine. If p+? and p-d may include other already changed variable, current value used.

Can we replace loop with vectorized slices for speed? For both region:

m = both

pos_slice = W[2*arange(1,m+1)-1]? Strided slice. W[1:2*m+1:2]? If m>0: prod = h[p+1:p+m] + h[p-1:p-1-m:-1]? Need descending left reversed. left_products = h[p-1:p-m-1:-1] length m (indices p-1 down p-m). prod = h[p+1:p+1+m] + h[p-1:p-m-1:-1]

```
For only right from d_start = both+1 to right_count:

This vectorized update with slices and numpy operations O(n) C, much faster. Use this! Need ensure slicing when lengths 0.

Let's define `apply_delta(W,h,p,delta, dx)`:
```
n = len(h)
a = h[p]
W[0] += dx * (delta * (1.0 - 2.0*a - delta))
h[p] = a + delta
c = dx * delta
right = n - 1 - p
left = p
both = min(right, left)
if both > 0:
    prod = h[p+1:p+1+both] + h[p-1:p-1-both:-1]
    # pos indices 1,3,...,2*both-1
    W[1:2*both+1:2] += c * (1.0 - prod)
    W[2:2*both+2:2] += c * (1.0 - prod)
if right > left:
    start = left + 1
    end = right
    length = right - left
    # d from start to end
    prod = h[p+start : p+end+1]
    pos_start = 2*start - 1
    W[pos_start : pos_start + 2*length : 2] += c * (1.0 - prod)
    neg_start = pos_start + 1  # since neg index for d=start is 2*start = pos_start+1
    W[neg_start : neg_start + 2*length : 2] -= c * prod
elif left > right:
    start = right + 1
    end = left
    length = left - right
    # d descending values: d=start -> p-start down to p-end. Need prod aligned d=start..end.
    # indices: p-start, p-(start+1), ..., p-end. Slice start +1? In Python: h[p-start : p-end-1 : -1]
    prod = h[p-start : p-end-1 : -1]
    pos_start = 2*start - 1
    W[pos_start : pos_start + 2*length : 2] -= c * prod
    neg_start = pos_start + 1
    W[neg_start : neg_start + 2*length : 2] += c * (1.0 - prod)
```
Check slice endpoints:
- pos length l indices: first=2*start-1, last=2*end =? stride 2 -> number l: first +2(l-1)=2*start-1+2(end-start+1)-2=2end-? compute: 2start-1+2end-2start+2-2=2end-1, which is pos index (2d-1). Need stop exclusive +2 -> pos_start+2*l = 2start-1+2end-2start+2=2end+1. Good `pos_start : pos_start+2*length : 2` includes last 2end-1.
- neg first=2start, last=2end; stop +2 =2end+2.
For both slice length both: start=1, end=both. pos stop pos_start(1)+2*both=2both+1 includes last 2both-1. neg start2 stop 2+2both=2both+2 includes last 2both. Good.

This update uses `c=dx*delta`; can be negative if delta negative. The formulas: Wpos += dx*delta*(linpos - prod). If c negative, adding works.

Let's design with scipy.milp. Variables:
0..n-1: x_i integer 0-1
n: M continuous (lower 0)
n+1 .. n+n_pairs-1: y_pos (binary? continuous 0-1)
... y_neg

For n=80, m=3160, vars=6401.
Constraints:
1. sum x_i = n/2 equality.
2. For each distance d=1..n-1: sum y_pos_d - M ≤0; sum y_neg_d - M ≤0.
3. For each y pos_{i,d} (i=0..n-1-d): constraints:
   y ≤ x_i : y - x_i ≤0
   y ≤ 1 - x_{i+d} : y + x_{i+d} ≤1
   y ≥ x_i - x_{i+d} : -y + x_i - x_{i+d} ≤0
 y bounds 0,1.
For y_neg_{i,d}:
   z ≤ x_{i+d}
   z ≤ 1 - x_i
   z ≥ x_{i+d} - x_i
Need build sparse rows. Could avoid storing all linearization constraints by using `y` as auxiliary with inequality; yes.

This is large but sparse. We can build using scipy.sparse.lil_matrix? For 6400 vars, 25k constraints, lil okay. But in code, fill loops O(n^2) Python, okay for n≤80. For n=120, m=7140, vars 14401, constraints ~43k; might be heavy but maybe okay for one solve. Time unknown. Use n=64/80.

Equality sum x: row with ones in x positions, ub=n/2, lb=n/2.
Distance constraints: for each y_pos_d, coefficients +1; M -1. Row count 2(n-1). Need map y variable indices.
Linearization rows: For each pos y index iy, row coefficients: y - x_i ≤0, y + x_j ≤1, -y + x_i - x_j ≤0. But `milp` supports constraints with different bounds? We can combine into a single sparse matrix with `lb` and `ub` arrays. For each row one constraint.

Now continuous optimization using incremental pair moves. This might be most likely to improve current. Let's design carefully.

Represent h any length n. We can start from parent and maybe binary/interpolated. Use incremental local search for continuous:
- Choose a target pair move (i,j,delta). We can generate candidates via:
  1. Greedy gradient: Compute active lags weights (smooth). For each coordinate i, raw feasible gradient g_feas. Move mass from positions with negative gradient? Actually gradient of objective: decrease by moving in negative gradient. Under sum constraint, choose i where g_i low (want increase?) Let's derive: Direction d = - (g - mean(g)). Coordinates with high (g-mean) decreased, low increased. So pair from high d to low d. We can pick top k negative direction and top k positive direction. But local gradient may be noisy. Use pairs (p, q) where p has high value? Not necessarily.
  2. Random pairs biased by values: choose i with high h, j with low h (to increase bistability) and vice versa.
  3. Pairs differing by active lag distances: If a lag pos active (W_pos_d high), to reduce it, need make h_i and h_{i+d} both high or both low? W_pos = h_i(1-h_j); reduce by decreasing h_i or increasing h_j. For a specific active pair (i,i+d) where h_i high and h_j low, move mass i->j. For neg active, move j->i. We can enumerate top offending pairs from active lags. This targeted move likely effective.

Targeted pair moves from active lags:
- At current h, compute W and identify active lags (top e.g., 20 or those within 1% of max).
- For each active lag d and orientation pos/neg:
   Pos: W_pos_d = dx∑_{i=0}^{n-d-1} h_i(1-h_{i+d}). The contributions c_i = h_i(1-h_{i+d}). Pick pairs with high contribution. If h_i > h_{i+d}, move mass from i to i+d (delta positive? transfer from i to j: h_i decreases, h_j increases) to reduce this term. If h_i < h_{i+d}, maybe moving opposite could reduce elsewhere? For target, move from high contributor source to deficit.
   Neg: contribution h_{i+d}(1-h_i); if h_{i+d} > h_i, move from i+d to i.
- Candidate set: for each active lag, sort top L pairs by contribution, propose transfer epsilon from source to target. Collect unique. Try them. This focuses.

Delta selection: For each source s, target t, possible transfer amount up to min(h_s, 1-h_t) for s->t; or reverse. We can try a few values: e.g., 0.25,0.5,1 of max? Continuous. Try `eps_vals = [0.01,0.02,0.04,0.08,0.15,0.3]` times max possible? Since local, small. Also for binary swap max=1.
- Use incremental update and evaluate. We can choose best among a small set.
- After trying candidate set, maybe random residual.

Let's design a local polishing loop:
```
def polish_continuous(h, W, C, seed, time_limit):
  n=len(h); dx=2/n
  rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
  last_improve=0
  while time remaining:
    # identify active components
    maxC = np.max(W)
    # active thresholds: lags where W > maxC - tol, tol e.g. 0.0005 or 0.002? C ~0.38. Use all top 30.
    top_idx = np.argpartition(W, -30)[-30:] (if W length). For each idx:
      if idx==0 skip? For zero active, maybe smooth (reduce fractional).
      else compute d, orient.
    candidate pairs = set
    for idx in top_idx:
       if idx==0: zero lag: fractional entries; pair high h and low h? reduce variance by moving mass to extremes? Actually V0=∑h(1-h); to reduce, push toward 0/1. We can propose moves from median values to extremes.
       else:
          if pos idx odd: d=(idx+1)//2
             contrib = h[:n-d] * (1 - h[d:])
          if neg: d=idx//2; contrib = h[d:] * (1 - h[:n-d])? Source high (right), target left.
          # find top m contributions (e.g., 5) and propose move from source to target.
    random add some pairs.
    for each (s,t,eps): try
       eps sign? source to target. We'll define transfer from a to b by δ_a = -eps, δ_b = +eps (requires 0<= h[a]-eps, h[b]+eps <=1)
       Also reverse b->a maybe.
    If any improved, update h,W,C, last_improve=0, recompute W perhaps after many accepts.
    Else perturb: add noise, project, recompute W, maybe lower? If not improve within X tries, restart with random or previous best?
```

Let's detail binary local search algorithm.

`binary_local_search(h, n, seed, time_limit, target_C=None)`:
- h binary float, W, C.

Use arrays and after accept swap values and swap positions in lists if needed? But if h[j] becomes 1, h[i] 0; lists of ones: replace i with j in list if indices known? We can maintain two `set` and `rng.choice(list(ones))` O(n) each unacceptable. Use arrays and when values swap, we can update by swapping values in one/zero lists:
   ones list contains i (since h_i=1); zeros list contains j. After swap, h_i=0,h_j=1. We need replace i with j in ones, and j with i in zeros. If we know their positions, O(n) to find. Not worth. Instead for sampling, keep full h and choose random indices; if one/zero mismatch skip. For n small, expected half correct. Or maintain `ones` as list with duplicates? - Candidate generation: Random swaps plus targeted active. Use active to propose moves.

Targeted active for binary:
For active pos d, contributions = h[:n-d]*(1-h[d:]). Since binary, high contributions are 1 where h_i=1,h_j=0. For each such source i,target j=i+d. Move source->target (swap). For active neg d, source j=i+d, target i. So for top active lags, we can collect top pairs with contribution 1. Try swap from source to target. This directly reduces that term. This could be very efficient.

`project_box_sum(h,target)`:
- Clip 0,1.
- Use bisection or water-filling to exactly satisfy sum in box:
   We need find α such that ∑ clip(h+λ,0,1) = target? Not simple if λ uniform. Simpler iterative:
```
for 20:
  diff = target - sum
  mask = (h<1-1e-12) if diff>0 else (h>1e-12)
  if any: h[mask]+=diff/select count
  else: # if at bound cannot exact? adjust evenly
  clip
```
This can fail if diff too large but okay for small noise. We can use after init from interpolation where sum close.
Better: exact projection to box+sum via sorting:
   Find λ and μ? We need project y onto {0≤x≤1, sum target}. This is P_Σ(y). Algorithm:

x = np.sort(y)
cum = np.cumsum(x)

```

Interpolation to other n:
```
def resample_h(h, n):
  x = (np.arange(len(h))+0.5)*2/len(h)
  if n==len(h): return h.copy()
  xn=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n
  y=np.interp(xn,x,h, period? no)
  return project_box_sum(y,n/2)
```
For binary, interpolation yields fractional; project.

def evaluate_c5_fast(h):
    n=len(h)

def solve_binary_milp(n, time_limit_s):
    try:
       from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    except Exception:
       return None
    if n%2 !=0: return None
    m = n*(n-1)//2
    V = n + 1 + 2*m
    c = np.zeros(V); c[n] = 1.0
    integ = np.zeros(V); integ[:n]=1
    lb = np.zeros(V); ub = np.ones(V); ub[n]=np.inf; # y default 1? x int, y 0-1, M inf
    # Actually x ub 1, M ub inf, y ub1
    # Build A
    pos_base = n+1
    neg_base = pos_base + m
    # precompute before(d)
    def before(d): # but top-level no closures? We can use math inside function; nested functions are closures? Rule no closures or lambdas. But local nested function is closure? It doesn't capture? It captures n? If inner function, likely not allowed. Avoid nested functions.

```
def evaluate_transfer(h,W,dx,s,t,eps, threshold):
    if s==t or eps <= 1e-12: return None
    if h[s] < eps-1e-12 or h[t] > 1-eps-1e-12: return None
    h2=h.copy(); W2=W.copy()
    apply_delta(W2,h2,s,-eps,dx)
    apply_delta(W2,h2,t,eps,dx)
    incC=float(np.max(W2))
    if incC >= C_quick + quick_tol: return None
    W2=recompute_W(h2)
    C2=float(np.max(W2))
    if C2 <= C_quick + accept_tol:
       # ensure h2 within bounds maybe clip and project? apply should keep due check; but roundoff could outside by 1e-15
       h2 = np.clip(h2,0,1)
       W2 = recompute_W(h2)
       C2 = max(W2)
       return (C2,h2,W2)
    return None
```

def local_search(h, seed, deadline, binary=False, reserve=10, max_candidate=80):
 h = np.asarray(h,float).copy()
 n=len(h); target=n/2
 if binary:
    # binarize top n/2 values
    idx=np.argsort(-h)[:n//2]
    h=np.zeros(n); h[idx]=1
 else:
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
 dx=2/n
 W=recompute_W(h); C=max(W)
 best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C
 rng=RandomState(seed)
 no_acc=0
 attempts=0
 while time.time() < deadline - reserve:
    # generate moves
    moves = generate_moves(h,W,rng,binary,max_candidate)
    acc_count=0
    for (s,t,eps) in moves:
       res = evaluate_transfer(h,W,dx,s,t,eps,C, accept_tol)
       if res:
          C2=h2,W2
          # optional SA accept if C2 < C or equal or (worsen small with prob)
          if C2 < C + 1e-12:
             h,W,C = ...
             acc_count+=1
          elif binary and C2 == C ... etc
          if C < best_C ...
       attempts+=1
       if attempts % 200==0: recompute current? Could be drift from accepted? We set W from recompute in evaluate for accepted, so no drift. If equal accepted similarly recompute. Good.
    if acc_count==0:
       no_acc +=1
       # perturb if no_acc>=3
       if no_acc >= 3:
           if binary:
              # random several swaps random accept nonworse? or restart from best
              h=best_h.copy(); W=recompute_W(h); C=best_C
              # maybe random flip maintaining count
           else:
              noise = (0.01 + 0.005*no_acc) * rng.normal(size=n)
              h2=project_box_sum(h+noise,target); W2=recompute_W(h2); C2=max
              if C2 < best_C + 0.02:
                 h,W,C=h2,W2,C2
              else:
                 h=best_h.copy(); W=recompute_W(h); C=best_C
           no_acc=0
    if C <= target_c maybe break? 
 return best_h,best_C
```

def generate_binary_moves(h,W,rng,limit=120):
 moves=[]; seen=set()
 L=len(W); k=min(L,40)
 top = np.argpartition(W,-k)[-k:]
 for idx in top:
   if idx==0: continue
   if idx & 1: d=(idx+1)//2; m=n-d
      bad = np.where((h[:m] > 0.5) & (h[d:] < 0.5))[0]
      if bad.size:
         smp = rng.choice(bad, size=min(4,bad.size), replace=False)
         for i in smp: add (i,i+d,1.0)
   else: d=idx//2; m=n-d
      bad = np.where((h[d:] > 0.5) & (h[:m] < 0.5))[0]
      bad source i+d target i
 # random
 for _ in range(limit//2):
    s=rng.randint(n); t=rng.randint(n)
    if h[s]>0.5 and h[t]<0.5: add (s,t,1.0)
    elif h[t]>0.5 and h[s]<0.5: add (t,s,1.0)
 return unique limit
```

Let's write MILP robust:
```
def solve_binary_milp(n, time_limit_s):
    try:
       from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
       from scipy.sparse import lil_matrix, csr_matrix
    except Exception:
       return None
    if n%2 !=0 or n<16: return None
    m = n*(n-1)//2
    total_vars = n + 1 + 2*m
    c = np.zeros(total_vars); c[n]=1.0
    integ = np.zeros(total_vars); integ[:n]=1
    lb_var = np.zeros(total_vars); ub_var = np.ones(total_vars)
    ub_var[n] = np.inf
    num_rows = 1 + 2*(n-1) + 6*m
    A = lil_matrix((num_rows,total_vars), dtype=float)
    row_lb = -np.inf*np.ones(num_rows)
    row_ub = np.zeros(num_rows)
    # equality
    for i in range(n): A[0,i]=1.0
    row_lb[0]=n/2.0; row_ub[0]=n/2.0
    row=1
    pos_base = n+1; neg_base=pos_base+m
    for d in range(1,n):
       before=(d-1)*n - (d-1)*d//2
       start=pos_base+before
       for i in range(n-d): A[row,start+i]=1.0
       A[row,n] = -1.0
       row_ub[row]=0.0; row_lb[row]=-np.inf
       row+=1
       start=neg_base+before
       for i in range(n-d): A[row,start+i]=1.0
       A[row,n]=-1.0
       row_ub[row]=0
       row+=1
    for d ... linearization...
```
For rows with ub 0, we set row_ub already 0. For z ≤ 1 row set ub=1.
Linearization:
```
    for d ...:
       sr=pos_base+before
       for i in range(n-d):
          iy=sr+i; xi=i; xj=i+d
          A[row,iy]=1.0; A[row,xi]=-1.0; row_ub[row]=0; row+=1
          A[row,iy]=1.0; A[row,xj]=1.0; row_ub[row]=1; row+=1
          A[row,iy]=-1.0; A[row,xi]=1.0; A[row,xj]=-1.0; row_ub[row]=0; row+=1
       sr=neg_base+before
       for i:
          iy=sr+i; xi=i; xj=i+d
          A[row,iy]=1; A[row,xj]=-1; row_ub=0 # z <= xj
          A[row,iy]=1; A[row,xi]=1; row_ub=1 # z <=1-xi
          A[row,iy]=-1; A[row,xj]=1; A[row,xi]=-1; row_ub=0 # z >= xj - xi
```
Check neg linear lower: z ≥ x_j - x_i => -z + x_j - x_i ≤0. Yes row: -iy + xj - xi ≤0.
For pos lower: y ≥ x_i - x_j => -y + x_i - x_j ≤0. Good.
Distance constraint row has `row_lb` -inf, `row_ub` 0. Since initialized row_lb -inf, fine. For equality set.
After building, `A = csr_matrix(A)`. Some rows maybe all zero? no.
`linear = LinearConstraint(A, row_lb, row_ub)`.
`bounds = Bounds(lb_var, ub_var)`.
`options = {}`; try options time_limit. If `time_limit_s` integer. `res = milp(c, constraints=linear, integrality=integ, bounds=bounds, options={'time_limit': int(time_limit_s)})`. Some HiGHS may not accept 'time_limit'? Could be `time_limit` accepted. If error, call without options.
If res.success and res.x not None:

     h=rx.astype(float)

     return h,c
  else maybe if some x near 0/1 and sum off, adjust top n/2? Use res.x as scores: idx top n/2; h, compute C. If C finite and count sum okay, return. `evaluate_transfer` signature:
```
def evaluate_transfer(h,W,C,dx,s,t,eps,allow_equal):
   if ... return None
   h2 = h.copy(); W2 = W.copy()
   apply_delta(W2,h2,s,-eps,dx); apply_delta(W2,h2,t,eps,dx)
   incC = float(np.max(W2))
   if incC > C + 1e-7:
        return None
   W2 = recompute_W(h2)
   C2 = float(np.max(W2))
   if C2 < C - 1e-12 or (allow_equal and C2 <= C + 1e-12):
       h2 = np.clip(h2,0,1)
       h2 = project_box_sum(h2, len(h2)/2)
       W2 = recompute_W(h2)
       C2 = float(np.max(W2))
       return C2,h2,W2
   return None
```

def unique_moves(moves, limit):
 out=[]; seen=set()
 for mv in moves:
   s,t,e = mv
   if s==t or e<=0: continue
   key=(int(s),int(t),round(float(e),6))
   if key in seen: continue
   seen.add(key); out.append(mv)
   if len(out)>=limit: break
 return out
```

Continuous generate moves code specifics:
```
def generate_moves(h,W,rng,binary):
    n=len(h); moves=[]
    L=len(W)
    if L==0: return moves
    if binary:
       k=min(L, 30)
    else:
       k=min(L, 35)
    if k <= 0: return moves
    top = np.argpartition(W, -k)[-k:]
    # For continuous, maybe use top values with threshold close to max to avoid active? argpartition arbitrary among top k; okay.
    # sort top by W desc maybe
    top = top[np.argsort(W[top])[::-1]]
    for idx in top:
       if idx == 0:
          if not binary:
             # zero: add high-low pairs
             high = np.argsort(-h)[:8]
             low = np.argsort(h)[:8]
             for b in high:
                for a in low:
                   if a==b: continue
                   if h[b] > h[a]:
                      maxeps=min(h[b],1-h[a])
                      if maxeps>1e-5: add_eps_candidates(moves, a,b,maxeps,binary)
          continue
       if idx & 1:
           d=(idx+1)//2; m=n-d
           if m <=0: continue
           # Select sec top contributions
           if binary:
              bad = np.where((h[:m] > 0.5) & (h[d:] < 0.5))[0]
              if bad.size:
                 sec=min(4,bad.size)
                 if sec > 0:
                    samp = rng.choice(bad, sec, replace=False)
                    for i in samp:
                        s=int(i); t=int(i+d); e=1.0
                        if h[s] > h[t]: moves.append((s,t,e))
           else:
              contrib = h[:m]*(1.0-h[d:])
              sec=min(6,m)
              if sec>0:
                 if m==sec: inds=np.arange(m)
                 else: inds=np.argpartition(contrib, -sec)[-sec:]
                 inds=inds[np.argsort(contrib[inds])[::-1]]
                 for i in inds:
                    if float(h[i]) > float(h[i+d]) + 1e-9:
                       maxeps = min(h[i], 1.0 - h[i+d])
                       if maxeps>1e-6: add_continuous_eps(moves,i,i+d,maxeps)
       else:
           d=idx//2; m=n-d
           if m<=0: continue
           if binary:
              bad = np.where((h[d:] > 0.5) & (h[:m] < 0.5))[0]
              if bad.size:
                 sec=min(4,bad.size); samp=rng.choice(...)
                 for i in samp:
                    if h[i+d] > h[i]: moves.append((int(i+d),int(i),1.0))
           else:
              contrib = h[d:]*(1.0-h[:m])
              sec=min(6,m)
              inds=top...
              for i in inds:
                 if h[i+d] > h[i] + 1e-9:
                    maxeps = min(h[i+d],1-h[i]); add
    # random moves
    n_rand = 30 if binary else 50
    for _ in range(n_rand):
       s=int(rng.randint(n)); t=int(rng.randint(n))
       if s==t: continue
       if h[s] > h[t]:
          maxeps = min(h[s],1-h[t])
          if binary: if maxeps>0.99: moves.append((s,t,1.0))
          else: add_continuous_eps(moves,s,t,maxeps)
       elif h[t] > h[s]:
          if binary: if h[t]-h[s]>0.99: moves.append((t,s,1.0))
          else: add_continuous_eps(moves,t,s,min(h[t],1-h[s]))
       else:
          # equal, tiny random?
          pass
    # ensure limit
    return unique_moves(moves, 200)
```

def add_continuous_eps_candidates(moves,s,t,maxeps):
    if maxeps <= 1e-8: return
    vals = [maxeps, 0.5*maxeps, 0.2*maxeps, 0.05*maxeps]
    for a in (0.01,0.02,0.04):
       if a < maxeps: vals.append(a)
    for e in vals:
       if 0 < e <= maxeps + 1e-12:
          moves.append((s,t,float(min(e,maxeps))))

Now, `local_search` current W update: After accept, if C2 < best_C, `best_h = h2; best_C=C2`. We don't need best_W. At perturb, if best_h maybe have no W; recompute.

Pseudo local_search final:
```
def local_search(h, seed, deadline, binary=False, reserve=8):
    n=len(h)
    if binary:
        h = binarize_scores(h, n)
    else:
        h = project_box_sum(h, n/2.0)
    dx=2.0/n
    W=recompute_W(h); C=float(np.max(W))
    best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C
    rng=RandomState(seed)
    no_acc=0
    acc_count=0
    iter_count=0
    while time.time() < deadline - reserve:
       iter_count +=1
       moves=generate_moves(h,W,rng,binary)
       accepted_any=False
       for mv in moves:
           if time.time() >= deadline - reserve: break
           s,t,e=mv
           res=evaluate_transfer(h,W,C,dx,s,t,e,allow_equal=True, eps_exact=(binary or e>=1e-3))
           if res is None: continue
           C2,h2,W2=res
           if C2 < C - 1e-12:
               accept=True
           else:
               if C2 <= C + 1e-12:
                   # accept equal sometimes
                   accept = bool(rng.random() < (0.5 if binary else 0.2))
               else:
                   accept=False
           if accept:
               h,W,C = h2,W2,C2
               accepted_any=True
               if C < best_C - 1e-12:
                   best_C=C; best_h=h2.copy()
       if accepted_any:
           no_acc=0
       else:
           no_acc +=1
           if no_acc >= 2:
               # perturb current
               if binary:
                  scores = best_h + 0.3*rng.uniform(size=n)
                  h2=binarize_scores(scores,n)
               else:
                  noise_scale = 0.02 + 0.005*min(no_acc,10)
                  h2 = project_box_sum(h + noise_scale*rng.normal(size=n), n/2)
               W2 = recompute_W(h2); C2=float(np.max(W2))
               # Accept perturb if not too far from best
               if C2 <= best_C + 0.03 or rng.random() < 0.1:
                   h,W,C = h2,W2,C2
               else:
                   h = best_h.copy(); W=recompute_W(h); C=best_C
               no_acc=0
    # final recompute best
    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, n/2)
    best_C = float(evaluate_c5(np.clip(best_h,0,1)))
    return best_h,best_C
```
But using `evaluate_c5` (np.correlate) final may differ from W max slightly. Good.

```
def smooth_gradient(h,beta,top_k=30):
  n=len(h); dx=2/n; W=recompute_W(h)
  if top_k < len(W): idx = np.argpartition(W,-top_k)[-top_k:] else all
  vals=W[idx]; m=vals.max(); shift=beta*(vals-m); shift clip; ew=exp; z=sum; weights=ew/z
  g=np.zeros(n)
  # zero if selected? need mapping idx to weight. Build dict? top_k small; loop.
  for idx,wi in zip(idx,weights):
     w = wi*dx
     if idx==0:
        gx = np.zeros(n); gx[:] = 1.0 - 2.0*h; g += w*gx
     elif idx & 1: # pos d=idx? Wait layout: odd idx=2d-1 => d=(idx+1)//2
        d=(idx+1)//2; right=n-d
        # linpos i<right
        g[:n-d] += w
        prod = np.zeros(n)
        if n-d>0:
           # right neighbor for i<n-d: h[i+d]
           g[:n-d] -= w*h[d::? h[d:n] length n-d]
           g[n-d?] no.
        # left for i>=d
        g[d:] -= w*h[:-d]
     else: neg d=idx//2
        g[d:] += w
        g[:n-d] -= w*h[d:]
        g[d:] -= w*h[:-d]
  # feasible tangent: g -= mean(g)
  return g - g.mean()
```

def gradient_step(h, W, C, dx, beta, top_k, step_candidates):
   n=len(h); g=smooth_gradient(h,beta,top_k)
   gnrm=np.linalg.norm(g)
   if gnrm <1e-12: return None
   direction = -g/gnrm
   best_res=None
   for amp in [0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]:
       h2=project_box_sum(h + amp*direction, n/2)
       W2=recompute_W(h2); C2=max
       if C2 < C - 1e-10: best...
   return best
```

Code for smooth_gradient:
```
def smooth_gradient(h, beta, top_k=40):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    W = recompute_W(h)
    L = len(W)
    top_k = int(min(max(1,top_k), L))
    if top_k < L:
       idx = np.argpartition(W, -top_k)[-top_k:]
    else:
       idx = np.arange(L)
    vals = W[idx]
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    sh = beta*(vals-m)
    sh = np.where(sh > -700, sh, -700)
    ew = np.exp(sh)
    z = float(np.sum(ew))
    if z <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    weights = ew/z
    g = np.zeros(n)
    inv = 1.0
    for ii in range(len(idx)):
       wi = weights[ii]
       if wi < 1e-14: continue
       idxi = int(idx[ii]); w = wi*dx
       if idxi == 0:
          g += w*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
       elif idxi & 1:
          d=(idxi+1)//2
          if d <=0 or d>=n: continue
          rr=n-d
          g[:rr] += w
          g[:rr] -= w*h[d:d+rr]
          g[d:] -= w*h[:d]
       else:
          d=idxi//2
          if d <=0 or d>=n: continue
          rr=n-d
          g[d:] += w
          g[:rr] -= w*h[d:d+rr]
          g[d:] -= w*h[:d]
    g -= np.mean(g)
    return -g
```
For neg, product same; okay.
Note for negative for i>=d: +1 linear; product -h[i-d]. Good.
But for V0 if sum constraint tangent subtract mean, derivative should maybe -2h not 1-2h? The constant 1 will be removed by mean if sum fixed? Since g -= mean, adding constant to g removed. So could use `g -= 2w h` only. Both okay.

Gradient polish:
```
def gradient_polish(h, seed, deadline, reserve=8):
   n=len(h); h=project_box_sum(h,n/2)
   W=recompute_W(h); C=...
   best...
   rng=RandomState(seed)
   betas=[30,80,200,500,1000,2000]
   amps=[0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002]
   stall=0
   while time < deadline:
      improved=False
      for beta in betas:
          if time... break
          direction = smooth_gradient(h,beta,top_k=50)
          norm=np.linalg.norm(direction)
          if norm < 1e-12: continue
          direction = direction/norm
          for amp in amps:
              if time... break
              h2=project_box_sum(h+amp*direction, n/2)
              W2=recompute_W(h2); c2=...
              if c2 < C - 1e-10:
                 h,W,C = h2,W2,c2; improved=True
                 if C<best...
                 break
      if improved: stall=0
      else:
         stall+=1
         if stall >=3:
            # noise or reset
            h2=project_box_sum(h+0.03*rng.normal(size=n), n/2); ... if c2 < best+0.02 accept else reset
            stall=0
   return best
```
This recomputes W many times for each amplitude; n=160, okay. Could be slow if many. Limit time.

```
def get_descending(arr, a, b):
    if b <= 0:
        return arr[:a+1][::-1]
    else:
        return arr[b:a+1][::-1]
```

Let's write `project_box_sum` robust:
```
def project_box_sum(y,target):
    y=np.asarray(y,dtype=float).copy()
    n=y.size
    y=np.clip(y,0.0,1.0)
    # if current sum exact
    # find lambda in [-1,1]? y in [0,1], target in [0,n]
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    # If y has out of [0,1], before clip? after clip.
    def f? no closures; just loop:
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(y+mid,0,1))
       if s > target: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    x=np.clip(y+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # Correct residual
    for _ in range(10):
       diff = target - np.sum(x)
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       if diff > 0:
          mask = x < 1.0 - 1e-15
       else:
          mask = x > 1e-15
       cnt = np.count_nonzero(mask)
       if cnt > 0:
          x[mask] += diff / cnt
          x=np.clip(x,0,1)
       else:
          # cannot adjust inside bounds, distribute all with clip
          x[:] += diff / n
          x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    # final clip and if diff remains, adjust unbounded? target within [0,n]
    if abs(target - np.sum(x)) > 1e-9:
        x = np.full(n, target/n)
        x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    return x
```
If y sum far, bisection finds lambda. If y after clip cannot reach target? target in [0,n]; should.

But bisection using y clipped to [0,1] and lambda range [-1,1] enough? If y all 0 and target n/2, need lambda=0.5 within. If y all1 and target n/2, lambda=-0.5. Good.
Max 70 loops O(n*70); for many perturbs could be cost. But okay. Maybe reduce 50.

For local_search binary accepted, `project_box_sum` still does bisection. Could be slow. We can make `project_box_sum` return quickly if y within bounds and sum diff small:
```
s=np.sum(y)
if np.all(y>=-1e-12)&... and abs(s-target)<1e-12: return clip
```
Good.

`apply_delta`: Use `dx` parameter; no target. It updates h and W. Need if delta zero. It may produce h slightly out; caller checks. Code:
```
def apply_delta(W,h,p,delta,dx):
    if abs(delta) < 1e-16: return
    n=len(h)
    a=h[p]
    W[0] += dx*delta*(1-2*a-delta)
    h[p] = a+delta
    c=dx*delta
    right=n-1-p; left=p; both = min(right,left)
    if both>0:
        right_part = h[p+1:p+1+both]
        b=p-both; aidx=p-1
        if b <=0:
            left_part = h[:aidx+1][::-1]
            # length both? If aidx=0, h[1:][::-1] length1; okay. But if h length larger and b<0? b=p-both, with both=min; both<=left=p, so b>=0.
        else:
            left_part = h[b:aidx+1][::-1]
        prod=right_part+left_part
        W[1:2*both+1:2] += c*(1.0 - prod)
        W[2:2*both+2:2] += c*(1.0 - prod)
    if right > left:
        start=left+1; end=right
        if end>=start:
            prod=h[p+start:p+end+1]
            pos_start=2*start-1; length=end-start+1
            W[pos_start:pos_start+2*length:2] += c*(1.0-prod)
            W[pos_start+1:pos_start+1+2*length:2] -= c*prod
    elif left > right:
        start=right+1; end=left
        if end>=start:
            aidx=p-start; bidx=p-end
            if bidx<=0:
                prod=h[:aidx+1][::-1]
            else:
                prod=h[bidx:aidx+1][::-1]
            length=end-start+1
            pos_start=2*start-1
            W[pos_start:pos_start+2*length:2] -= c*prod
            W[pos_start+1:pos_start+1+2*length:2] += c*(1.0-prod)
```
Check left length if bidx<=0: `h[:aidx+1][::-1]` length aidx+1 = p-start+1. But start = right+1, end=left. length=end-start+1 = left-right. Are these equal? p-start+1 = p-right. Since start=right+1. Need p-right = left-right? only if p=left? p=left when p>=right? In left-only region, left>p? We have left=p always! Wait I used left = p, right=n-1-p. For left-only, left>p? left variable is p. left>p? no, left equals p. Oops notation conflict. In update formula, `left` count = p (number of d with left neighbor, d≤p). It is not a position. In left-only, p < right? Actually left>p? If p > n-1-p, i.e., p>right, left count = p, right count = n-1-p. `start=right+1`, `end=left` (which is p). Then length = p-right. aidx=p-start = p-right-1 = right-1, bidx=p-end=0. aidx+1 = right. h[:right][::-1] length right. left-right? p? If p=7,n=10,right=2, length=5, aidx=4, aidx+1=5, length5. Good. In general if bidx=0 always in left-only? end=left=p, so bidx=0. Yes left-only includes all d up to p, so bidx 0. So `h[:aidx+1][::-1]` length aidx+1 = right? length= p-(right+1)+1 = p-right. aidx=p-(right+1)=p-right-1; +1=p-right. Good. The else bidx>0 will not happen for left-only because end=left=p => bidx=0. Wait what if we made a region only left d from `right+1` to `left`, and left=p, yes bidx=0. So else not used. But keep safe.
For both region, both=min(right,p). b=p-both. If p=3,right=5,both=3,b=0; if p=3,right=2,both=2,b=1>0. Good. Slices length both.
Right-only when right>left: right=n-1-p > p. start=p+1, end=n-1-p. prod length. pos_start=2p+1? if p=2,start3. Good.

Need be careful variable names `left` count; in slice for left-only, use `bidx = p - end`, which is 0. Fine.

`evaluate_transfer`: after apply, h2 values might be e.g. 1+1e-16. For binary rounding, threshold 0.5 works. For continuous, `np.clip`. Then project. Project uses bisection; if h2 already feasible, quick check.
```
def evaluate_transfer(h,W,C,dx,s,t,eps,binary):
   if s==t: return None
   e=abs(eps)
   # transfer s->t by eps positive. If eps negative? We'll only pass positive; handle sign? If eps<0, swap? Simpler if e <1e-12 return.
   if e < 1e-12: return None
   if eps < 0:
       # reverse
       s,t = t,s; eps = -eps
   if h[s] < eps - 1e-12 or h[t] > 1.0 - eps - 1e-12: return None
   h2 = h.copy(); W2 = W.copy()
   apply_delta(W2,h2,s,-eps,dx)
   apply_delta(W2,h2,t, eps,dx)
   incC = float(np.max(W2))
   if incC > C + 1e-7:
       return None
   if binary:
      h2 = np.where(h2 < 0.5, 0.0, 1.0)
   else:
      h2 = np.clip(h2,0.0,1.0)
      h2 = project_box_sum(h2, len(h2)*0.5)
   W2 = recompute_W(h2)
   C2 = float(np.max(W2))
   if C2 < C - 1e-13 or C2 <= C + 1e-12:
       return (C2,h2,W2)
   return None
```
For local_search, we then decide equal acceptance. It may return equal even if C2 > C by 1e-12. Good.

`gradient_polish` code:
```
def gradient_polish(h, seed, deadline, reserve=8):
    n=len(h); h=project_box_sum(h,n/2.0)
    W=recompute_W(h); C=float(np.max(W))
    best...
    rng=RandomState(seed)
    betas=(30,80,200,500,1000,2000)
    amps=(0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03,0.015,0.008,0.004)
    stall=0
    while time.time() < deadline - reserve:
       improved=False
       for beta in betas:
          if time.time() >= deadline-reserve: break
          direction = smooth_gradient(h,beta,50)
          norm=float(np.linalg.norm(direction))
          if norm <1e-12: continue
          direction = direction/norm
          for amp in amps:
              if time... break
              h2=project_box_sum(h + amp*direction, n/2.0)
              W2=recompute_W(h2); c2=...
              if c2 < C - 1e-10:
                  h,W,C = h2,W2,c2; improved=True; if C<best...; break
          if improved and C <= best_C + 1e-12:
              # maybe continue? We can break after one improvement per beta iteration? To use remaining time with beta, continue outer? Maybe skip rest beta to avoid too fast? We can `if improved: break` to recompute new direction.
              break
       if improved: stall=0; continue
       stall +=1
       if stall >= 3:
          noise=0.02+0.01*min(stall,5)
          h2=project_box_sum(h+noise*rng.normal(size=n), n/2.0)
          W2=recompute_W(h2); c2=...
          if c2 <= best_C + 0.02:
             h,W,C = h2,W2,c2
          else:
             h=best_h.copy(); W=recompute_W(h); C=best_C
          stall=0
    best_h=project_box_sum(best_h,n/2); best_C=evaluate_c5(best_h)
    return best_h,best_C
```
This recomputes W for each amplitude; maybe 6*7*beta ~84 recomputes per loop, too many. But n=160 recompute cheap; loops until stall. Could be okay. Limit time. Maybe amps fewer. Use 5 amps. Good.

Potential issue: `smooth_gradient` calls recompute_W itself; then gradient_polish computes W2 for each amplitude. So double. Accept.

- `Bounds(lb_var, ub_var)`; `ub_var[n]=np.inf`. For y variables ub 1.
- If n=20, m=190 rows ~1141, okay.

Potential bug in `smooth_gradient`: It subtracts product terms twice for i in overlap of right and left? For V_pos_d, derivative product with both neighbors: For i where both right and left exist, product includes h_i h_{i+d} and h_{i-d} h_i, so grad = linear +1 - h_{i+d} - h_{i-d}. Our code: `g[:rr] += w; g[:rr] -= w*h[d:d+rr]; g[d:] -= w*h[:d]`. For i in 0..min(rr,d)-1, both slices: +1 - h_{i+d} - h_{i-d}. Count correct. For i in overlap? yes.
For V_neg_d: linear +1 for i>=d; product same. code `g[d:] += w; g[:rr] -= w*h[d:d+rr]; g[d:] -= w*h[:d]`. For i>=d and i<rr (i in d..rr-1): +1 - h_{i+d} - h_{i-d}. Good. For i>=rr but <d? possible if d > rr? Right length rr=n-d. If d > rr, there is no overlap? If n small? For d > n-d, i in d..n-1 (left slice) and i in 0..n-d-1 none overlapping because d > n-d. code okay. For i in [d,n) gets +1 - h_{i-d}; product with right? i<rr false so no; correct.
V0 derivative: If sum constraint tangent, constant removed. Good.

But smooth_gradient selects top components including indices where W maybe lower but weights. If beta large, one component. The raw gradient has magnitude ~dx; normalized direction. Good.

Potential bug: In local_search, `moves = generate_moves(...)` may return moves with source,target and eps > maxeps if h values changed? h unchanged during generation. evaluate checks bounds.

Potential bug: In `generate_moves` for continuous, if h source high but target almost 1, maxeps small; fractions maybe duplicate tiny. Unique.

Potential bug: `unique_moves` rounds eps to 6 decimals; two different eps 0.1234565 and 0.1234561 same? okay.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    from scipy.sparse import lil_matrix, csr_matrix
    _HAS_MILP = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_MILP = False


def time_left(deadline):
    return max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.time())


def project_box_sum(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).copy()
    n = int(y.size)
    if n == 0:
        return y
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(np.sum(y))
    if abs(s - float(target)) < 1e-12:
        return y
    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s_mid = float(np.sum(np.clip(y + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s_mid > float(target):
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    x = np.clip(y + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(12):
        diff = float(target) - float(np.sum(x))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = x < 1.0 - 1e-15
        else:
            mask = x > 1e-15
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt > 0:
            x[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            x[:] += diff / float(n)
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(target) - float(np.sum(x))) > 1e-8:
        x = np.full(n, float(target) / float(n), dtype=float)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x


def binarize_scores(scores, n):
    scores = np.asarray(scores, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    if scores.size != n:
        if scores.size > n:
            scores = scores[:n]
        else:
            scores = np.pad(scores, (0, n - scores.size), mode="edge")
    k = int(round(n / 2.0))
    k = max(0, min(n, k))
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if k > 0:
        idx = np.argsort(-scores)[:k]
        h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def evaluate_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    val = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(val) * dx)


def recompute_W(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(h.size)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    S = float(np.sum(h))
    s2 = float(np.dot(h, h))
    aut_full = np.correlate(h, h, mode="full")
    aut = aut_full[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    prefix = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(h)))
    V0 = dx * (S - s2)
    W = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    W[0] = V0
    if n > 1:
        d = np.arange(1, n)
        pos = dx * (prefix[n - d] - aut[1:])
        neg = dx * (S - prefix[d] - aut[1:])
        W[1::2] = pos
        W[2::2] = neg
    return W


def apply_delta(W, h, p, delta, dx):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    n = len(h)
    p = int(p)
    a = float(h[p])
    W[0] += dx * delta * (1.0 - 2.0 * a - delta)
    h[p] = a + delta
    c = dx * delta
    right = n - 1 - p
    left = p
    both = right if right < left else left
    if both > 0:
        right_part = h[p + 1:p + 1 + both]
        aidx = p - 1
        bidx = p - both
        if bidx <= 0:
            left_part = h[:aidx + 1][::-1]
        else:
            left_part = h[bidx:aidx + 1][::-1]
        prod = right_part + left_part
        W[1:2 * both + 1:2] += c * (1.0 - prod)
        W[2:2 * both + 2:2] += c * (1.0 - prod)
    if right > left:
        start = left + 1
        end = right
        if end >= start:
            prod = h[p + start:p + end + 1]
            length = end - start + 1
            pos_start = 2 * start - 1
            W[pos_start:pos_start + 2 * length:2] += c * (1.0 - prod)
            W[pos_start + 1:pos_start + 1 + 2 * length:2] -= c * prod
    elif left > right:
        start = right + 1
        end = left
        if end >= start:
            aidx = p - start
            bidx = p - end
            if bidx <= 0:
                prod = h[:aidx + 1][::-1]
            else:
                prod = h[bidx:aidx + 1][::-1]
            length = end - start + 1
            pos_start = 2 * start - 1
            W[pos_start:pos_start + 2 * length:2] -= c * prod
            W[pos_start + 1:pos_start + 1 + 2 * length:2] += c * (1.0 - prod)


def evaluate_transfer(h, W, C, dx, s, t, eps, binary):
    s = int(s)
    t = int(t)
    eps = float(eps)
    if s == t:
        return None
    if eps < 0.0:
        s, t = t, s
        eps = -eps
    if eps < 1e-12:
        return None
    if float(h[s]) < eps - 1e-12 or float(h[t]) > 1.0 - eps - 1e-12:
        return None
    h2 = h.copy()
    W2 = W.copy()
    apply_delta(W2, h2, s, -eps, dx)
    apply_delta(W2, h2, t, eps, dx)
    incC = float(np.max(W2))
    if incC > float(C) + 1e-7:
        return None
    if binary:
        h2 = np.where(h2 < 0.5, 0.0, 1.0)
    else:
        h2 = np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0)
        h2 = project_box_sum(h2, float(len(h2)) * 0.5)
    W2 = recompute_W(h2)
    C2 = float(np.max(W2))
    if C2 < float(C) - 1e-13 or C2 <= float(C) + 1e-12:
        return (C2, h2, W2)
    return None


def add_continuous_eps_candidates(moves, s, t, maxeps):
    maxeps = float(maxeps)
    if maxeps <= 1e-8:
        return
    for f in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.04):
        e = f * maxeps
        if e > 1e-8 and e <= maxeps + 1e-12:
            moves.append((int(s), int(t), float(min(e, maxeps))))
    for e in (0.01, 0.02, 0.04, 0.08):
        if e < maxeps - 1e-12:
            moves.append((int(s), int(t), float(e)))


def unique_moves(moves, limit):
    out = []
    seen = set()
    for mv in moves:
        if len(out) >= limit:
            break
        s, t, e = mv
        s = int(s)
        t = int(t)
        e = float(e)
        if s == t or e <= 1e-12:
            continue
        if e > 1.0 + 1e-12:
            e = 1.0
        key = (s, t, round(e, 7))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        out.append((s, t, e))
    return out


def generate_moves(h, W, rng, binary):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    moves = []
    if n <= 1:
        return moves
    L = len(W)
    k = min(L, 35)
    if k <= 0:
        return moves
    if k < L:
        top = np.argpartition(W, -k)[-k:]
    else:
        top = np.arange(L)
    top = top[np.argsort(W[top])[::-1]]
    sec = 4 if binary else 6
    random_count = 35 if binary else 55
    for idx in top:
        idx = int(idx)
        if len(moves) >= 260:
            break
        if idx == 0:
            if binary:
                continue
            high = np.argsort(-h)[:10]
            low = np.argsort(h)[:10]
            for b in high:
                for a in low:
                    if a == b:
                        continue
                    if float(h[b]) > float(h[a]):
                        maxeps = min(float(h[b]), 1.0 - float(h[a]))
                        if maxeps > 1e-6:
                            add_continuous_eps_candidates(moves, a, b, maxeps)
                    if float(h[a]) > float(h[b]):
                        maxeps = min(float(h[a]), 1.0 - float(h[b]))
                        if maxeps > 1e-6:
                            add_continuous_eps_candidates(moves, b, a, maxeps)
            continue

        if idx & 1:
            d = (idx + 1) // 2
            m = n - d
            if m <= 0:
                continue
            if binary:
                bad = np.where((h[:m] > 0.5) & (h[d:] < 0.5))[0]
                if bad.size:
                    ssel = min(sec, int(bad.size))
                    if ssel == int(bad.size):
                        samp = bad
                    else:
                        samp = rng.choice(bad, size=ssel, replace=False)
                    for ii in samp:
                        ii = int(ii)
                        if float(h[ii]) > float(h[ii + d]):
                            moves.append((ii, ii + d, 1.0))
            else:
                contrib = h[:m] * (1.0 - h[d:])
                ssel = min(sec, m)
                if ssel > 0:
                    if ssel == m:
                        inds = np.arange(m)
                    else:
                        inds = np.argpartition(contrib, -ssel)[-ssel:]
                    inds = inds[np.argsort(contrib[inds])[::-1]]
                    for ii in inds:
                        ii = int(ii)
                        if float(h[ii]) > float(h[ii + d]) + 1e-10:
                            maxeps = min(float(h[ii]), 1.0 - float(h[ii + d]))
                            if maxeps > 1e-7:
                                add_continuous_eps_candidates(moves, ii, ii + d, maxeps)
        else:
            d = idx // 2
            m = n - d
            if d <= 0 or m <= 0:
                continue
            if binary:
                bad = np.where((h[d:] > 0.5) & (h[:m] < 0.5))[0]
                if bad.size:
                    ssel = min(sec, int(bad.size))
                    if ssel == int(bad.size):
                        samp = bad
                    else:
                        samp = rng.choice(bad, size=ssel, replace=False)
                    for ii in samp:
                        ii = int(ii)
                        if float(h[ii + d]) > float(h[ii]):
                            moves.append((ii + d, ii, 1.0))
            else:
                contrib = h[d:] * (1.0 - h[:m])
                ssel = min(sec, m)
                if ssel > 0:
                    if ssel == m:
                        inds = np.arange(m)
                    else:
                        inds = np.argpartition(contrib, -ssel)[-ssel:]
                    inds = inds[np.argsort(contrib[inds])[::-1]]
                    for ii in inds:
                        ii = int(ii)
                        if float(h[ii + d]) > float(h[ii]) + 1e-10:
                            maxeps = min(float(h[ii + d]), 1.0 - float(h[ii]))
                            if maxeps > 1e-7:
                                add_continuous_eps_candidates(moves, ii + d, ii, maxeps)

    for _ in range(random_count):
        if len(moves) >= 320:
            break
        s = int(rng.randint(n))
        t = int(rng.randint(n))
        if s == t:
            continue
        if float(h[s]) > float(h[t]):
            maxeps = min(float(h[s]), 1.0 - float(h[t]))
            if binary:
                if maxeps > 0.99:
                    moves.append((s, t, 1.0))
            else:
                add_continuous_eps_candidates(moves, s, t, maxeps)
        elif float(h[t]) > float(h[s]):
            maxeps = min(float(h[t]), 1.0 - float(h[s]))
            if binary:
                if maxeps > 0.99:
                    moves.append((t, s, 1.0))
            else:
                add_continuous_eps_candidates(moves, t, s, maxeps)

    if not binary:
        for _ in range(18):
            if len(moves) >= 360:
                break
            s = int(rng.randint(n))
            t = int(rng.randint(n))
            if s == t:
                continue
            if float(h[s]) <= float(h[t]):
                src = s
                tgt = t
            else:
                src = t
                tgt = s
            maxeps = min(float(h[src]), 1.0 - float(h[tgt]))
            if maxeps > 1e-6:
                add_continuous_eps_candidates(moves, src, tgt, maxeps)

    return unique_moves(moves, 240 if binary else 280)


def local_search(h, seed, deadline, binary=False, reserve=8):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1).copy()
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return h, 0.0
    target = float(n) * 0.5
    if binary and n % 2 == 0:
        h = binarize_scores(h, n)
    else:
        h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    W = recompute_W(h)
    C = float(np.max(W))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_C = C
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    no_acc = 0
    while time.time() < float(deadline) - reserve:
        moves = generate_moves(h, W, rng, binary)
        accepted_any = False
        for mv in moves:
            if time.time() >= float(deadline) - reserve:
                break
            s, t, e = mv
            res = evaluate_transfer(h, W, C, dx, s, t, e, binary)
            if res is None:
                continue
            C2, h2, W2 = res
            if C2 < C - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif C2 <= C + 1e-12:
                accept = bool(rng.random() < (0.55 if binary else 0.22))
            else:
                accept = False
            if accept:
                h, W, C = h2, W2, float(C2)
                accepted_any = True
                if C < best_C - 1e-12:
                    best_C = C
                    best_h = h.copy()
        if accepted_any:
            no_acc = 0
        else:
            no_acc += 1
            if no_acc >= 2:
                if binary:
                    scores = best_h + 0.35 * rng.uniform(size=n)
                    h2 = binarize_scores(scores, n)
                else:
                    scale = 0.025 + 0.005 * min(no_acc, 8)
                    h2 = project_box_sum(h + scale * rng.normal(size=n), target)
                W2 = recompute_W(h2)
                C2 = float(np.max(W2))
                if C2 <= best_C + 0.035 or rng.random() < 0.12:
                    h, W, C = h2, W2, C2
                else:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    W = recompute_W(h)
                    C = best_C
                no_acc = 0
    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, target)
    best_C = evaluate_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, best_C


def smooth_gradient(h, beta, top_k=45):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    W = recompute_W(h)
    L = len(W)
    top_k = int(max(1, min(top_k, L)))
    if top_k < L:
        idx = np.argpartition(W, -top_k)[-top_k:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    vals = W[idx]
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    sh = float(beta) * (vals - m)
    sh = np.where(sh > -700.0, sh, -700.0)
    ew = np.exp(sh)
    z = float(np.sum(ew))
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if z <= 0.0:
        return -g
    weights = ew / z
    for ii in range(len(idx)):
        wi = float(weights[ii])
        if wi < 1e-15:
            continue
        idxi = int(idx[ii])
        w = wi * dx
        if idxi == 0:
            g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif idxi & 1:
            d = (idxi + 1) // 2
            if d <= 0 or d >= n:
                continue
            rr = n - d
            g[:rr] += w
            g[:rr] -= w * h[d:d + rr]
            g[d:] -= w * h[:d]
        else:
            d = idxi // 2
            if d <= 0 or d >= n:
                continue
            rr = n - d
            g[d:] += w
            g[:rr] -= w * h[d:d + rr]
            g[d:] -= w * h[:d]
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    return -g


def gradient_polish(h, seed, deadline, reserve=8):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1).copy()
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return h, 0.0
    target = float(n) * 0.5
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    W = recompute_W(h)
    C = float(np.max(W))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_C = C
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    betas = (30, 80, 200, 500, 1000, 2000)
    amps = (0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008, 0.004)
    stall = 0
    while time.time() < float(deadline) - reserve:
        improved = False
        for beta in betas:
            if time.time() >= float(deadline) - reserve:
                break
            direction = smooth_gradient(h, float(beta), 55)
            nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(direction))
            if nrm < 1e-12:
                continue
            direction /= nrm
            for amp in amps:
                if time.time() >= float(deadline) - reserve:
                    break
                h2 = project_box_sum(h + amp * direction, target)
                W2 = recompute_W(h2)
                c2 = float(np.max(W2))
                if c2 < C - 1e-10:
                    h, W, C = h2, W2, c2
                    improved = True
                    if C < best_C - 1e-12:
                        best_C = C
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    break
            if improved:
                break
        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 3:
                scale = 0.025 + 0.01 * min(stall, 6)
                h2 = project_box_sum(h + scale * rng.normal(size=n), target)
                W2 = recompute_W(h2)
                c2 = float(np.max(W2))
                if c2 <= best_C + 0.025 or rng.random() < 0.15:
                    h, W, C = h2, W2, c2
                else:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    W = recompute_W(h)
                    C = best_C
                stall = 0
    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, target)
    best_C = evaluate_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, best_C


def resample_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1).copy()
    n0 = len(h)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0 or n0 == 0:
        return np.zeros(max(1, n), dtype=float)
    if n0 == n:
        return project_box_sum(h, float(n) * 0.5)
    xp = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n0))
    xn = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    y = np.interp(xn, xp, h)
    return project_box_sum(y, float(n) * 0.5)


def default_initial_h(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    scores = (0.5 + 0.48 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
              + 0.27 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
              + 0.16 * np.cos(6.0 * np.pi * x))
    return binarize_scores(scores, n)


def solve_binary_milp(n, time_limit_s):
    if not _HAS_MILP:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0 or n < 16 or n > 96:
        return None
    time_limit_s = max(1.0, float(time_limit_s))
    m = n * (n - 1) // 2
    n_y = 2 * m
    total_vars = n + 1 + n_y
    c = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=float)
    c[n] = 1.0
    integ = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=int)
    integ[:n] = 1
    vb = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=float)
    ub = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=float)
    ub[n] = np.inf
    num_rows = 1 + 2 * (n - 1) + 6 * m
    A = lil_matrix((num_rows, total_vars), dtype=float)
    row_lb = -np.inf * np.ones(num_rows, dtype=float)
    row_ub = np.zeros(num_rows, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        A[0, i] = 1.0
    row_lb[0] = float(n) * 0.5
    row_ub[0] = float(n) * 0.5
    r = 1
    pos_base = n + 1
    neg_base = pos_base + m
    for d in range(1, n):
        before = (d - 1) * n - (d - 1) * d // 2
        start = pos_base + before
        for i in range(n - d):
            A[r, start + i] = 1.0
        A[r, n] = -1.0
        row_lb[r] = -np.inf
        row_ub[r] = 0.0
        r += 1
        start = neg_base + before
        for i in range(n - d):
            A[r, start + i] = 1.0
        A[r, n] = -1.0
        row_lb[r] = -np.inf
        row_ub[r] = 0.0
        r += 1
    for d in range(1, n):
        before = (d - 1) * n - (d - 1) * d // 2
        start = pos_base + before
        for i in range(n - d):
            iy = start + i
            xi = i
            xj = i + d
            A[r, iy] = 1.0
            A[r, xi] = -1.0
            row_lb[r] = -np.inf
            row_ub[r] = 0.0
            r += 1
            A[r, iy] = 1.0
            A[r, xj] = 1.0
            row_lb[r] = -np.inf
            row_ub[r] = 1.0
            r += 1
            A[r, iy] = -1.0
            A[r, xi] = 1.0
            A[r, xj] = -1.0
            row_lb[r] = -np.inf
            row_ub[r] = 0.0
            r += 1
        start = neg_base + before
        for i in range(n - d):
            iy = start + i
            xi = i
            xj = i + d
            A[r, iy] = 1.0
            A[r, xj] = -1.0
            row_lb[r] = -np.inf
            row_ub[r] = 0.0
            r += 1
            A[r, iy] = 1.0
            A[r, xi] = 1.0
            row_lb[r] = -np.inf
            row_ub[r] = 1.0
            r += 1
            A[r, iy] = -1.0
            A[r, xj] = 1.0
            A[r, xi] = -1.0
            row_lb[r] = -np.inf
            row_ub[r] = 0.0
            r += 1
    A = csr_matrix(A)
    linear = LinearConstraint(A, row_lb, row_ub)
    constraints = Bounds(vb, ub)
    try:
        res = milp(c, constraints=linear, integrality=integ, bounds=constraints,
                   options={"time_limit": int(time_limit_s), "mip_rel_gap": 0.0})
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = milp(c, constraints=linear, integrality=integ, bounds=constraints)
        except Exception:
            return None
    if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if x.size >= n:
            scores = x[:n]
            h = binarize_scores(scores, n)
            cc = evaluate_c5(h)
            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < 0.5:
                return (h, float(cc))
    return None


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = max(float(budget_s), 1.0)
    reserve = max(3.0, 0.018 * budget)
    deadline = t0 + budget - reserve
    target_bound = 0.38085

    base_seed = int(seed)
    try:
        par_in = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
        if par_in.size == 0:
            raise ValueError("empty parent")
    except Exception:
        par_in = default_initial_h(160)

    par_n = int(par_in.size)
    par_h = project_box_sum(par_in, float(par_n) * 0.5)
    par_c = evaluate_c5(par_h)

    best_h = par_h.copy()
    best_C = float(par_c)
    best_n = par_n
    print("parent n={} C5={: .7f}".format(best_n, best_C), flush=True)

    if time_left(deadline) > 120:
        dd = min(deadline, time.time() + min(170.0, time_left(deadline) - 30.0))
        h2, c2 = gradient_polish(best_h, base_seed + 11, dd, reserve=6)
        if c2 < best_C - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, float(c2), int(len(h2))
            print("gradient polish C5={: .7f}".format(best_C), flush=True)

    if time_left(deadline) > 90:
        dd = min(deadline, time.time() + min(180.0, time_left(deadline) - 30.0))
        h2, c2 = local_search(best_h, base_seed + 23, dd, binary=False, reserve=6)
        if c2 < best_C - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, float(c2), int(len(h2))
            print("continuous local C5={: .7f}".format(best_C), flush=True)

    base_n = best_n if best_n % 2 == 0 else best_n + 1
    if base_n < 40:
        base_n = 40
    if base_n > 320:
        base_n = 320
    n_list = []
    for nn in (base_n, base_n - 16, base_n + 16, base_n - 32, base_n + 32,
               96, 128, 160, 192, 224, 256):
        nn = int(nn)
        if 32 <= nn <= 320 and nn % 2 == 0 and nn not in n_list:
            n_list.append(nn)
    n_list = n_list[:4]

    binary_chunks = (220.0, 140.0, 100.0, 70.0)
    for i, nn in enumerate(n_list):
        if time_left(deadline) < 55.0:
            break
        chunk = binary_chunks[i % len(binary_chunks)]
        if best_C <= target_bound:
            chunk = min(chunk, 70.0)
        chunk = min(chunk, time_left(deadline) - 8.0)
        if chunk < 20.0:
            continue
        hstart = resample_h(best_h, nn)
        dd = time.time() + chunk
        h2, c2 = local_search(hstart, base_seed + 101 + i, dd, binary=True, reserve=5)
        if c2 < best_C - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, float(c2), int(len(h2))
            print("binary local n={} C5={: .7f}".format(best_n, best_C), flush=True)

    if time_left(deadline) > 70.0:
        for ii, nn in enumerate([base_n] + [x for x in n_list[1:3] if x != base_n]):
            if time_left(deadline) < 55.0:
                break
            chunk = min(70.0, time_left(deadline) - 8.0)
            if best_C <= target_bound:
                chunk = min(chunk, 45.0)
            if chunk < 20.0:
                continue
            hrand = default_initial_h(nn)
            dd = time.time() + chunk
            h2, c2 = local_search(hrand, base_seed + 205 + ii, dd, binary=True, reserve=5)
            if c2 < best_C - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, float(c2), int(len(h2))
                print("random binary n={} C5={: .7f}".format(best_n, best_C), flush=True)

    if _HAS_MILP and time_left(deadline) > 160.0:
        for ii, nm in enumerate((80, 64)):
            if time_left(deadline) < 110.0:
                break
            tl = min(85.0, time_left(deadline) - 80.0)
            if tl < 25.0:
                continue
            out = solve_binary_milp(nm, tl)
            if out is None:
                continue
            hm, cm = out
            cm = float(cm)
            if cm < best_C - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_C, best_n = hm.copy(), cm, int(nm)
                print("MILP n={} C5={: .7f}".format(best_n, best_C), flush=True)
            if time_left(deadline) > 70.0:
                h2, c2 = local_search(hm, base_seed + 301 + ii, time.time() + min(50.0, time_left(deadline) - 30.0),
                                      binary=True, reserve=4)
                if c2 < best_C - 1e-12:
                    best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, float(c2), int(len(h2))
                    print("MILP binary polish C5={: .7f}".format(best_C), flush=True)
            if time_left(deadline) > 55.0:
                h2, c2 = local_search(hm, base_seed + 302 + ii, time.time() + min(45.0, time_left(deadline) - 30.0),
                                      binary=False, reserve=4)
                if c2 < best_C - 1e-12:
                    best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, float(c2), int(len(h2))
                    print("MILP continuous polish C5={: .7f}".format(best_C), flush=True)

    if time_left(deadline) > 70.0:
        dd = deadline
        h2, c2 = local_search(best_h, base_seed + 503, dd, binary=False, reserve=5)
        if c2 < best_C - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, float(c2), int(len(h2))
            print("final local C5={: .7f}".format(best_C), flush=True)

    if time_left(deadline) > 55.0:
        dd = min(deadline, time.time() + min(65.0, time_left(deadline) - 10.0))
        h2, c2 = gradient_polish(best_h, base_seed + 605, dd, reserve=4)
        if c2 < best_C - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, float(c2), int(len(h2))
            print("final gradient C5={: .7f}".format(best_C), flush=True)

    if time_left(deadline) > 90.0:
        seen_ns = {best_n}
        extra_ns = []
        for nn in (best_n - 16, best_n + 16, best_n - 32, best_n + 32, 160, 192, 224):
            nn = int(nn)
            if 32 <= nn <= 320 and nn % 2 == 0 and nn not in seen_ns:
                extra_ns.append(nn)
                seen_ns.add(nn)
                if len(extra_ns) >= 3:
                    break
        for i, nn in enumerate(extra_ns):
            if time_left(deadline) < 60.0:
                break
            chunk = min(60.0, time_left(deadline) - 10.0)
            if best_C <= target_bound:
                chunk = min(chunk, 35.0)
            if chunk < 20.0:
                continue
            hstart = resample_h(best_h, nn)
            dd = time.time() + chunk
            h2, c2 = local_search(hstart, base_seed + 701 + i, dd, binary=False, reserve=4)
            if c2 < best_C - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, float(c2), int(len(h2))
                print("extra resample n={} C5={: .7f}".format(best_n, best_C), flush=True)

    best_n = int(len(best_h))
    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, float(best_n) * 0.5)
    final_c = evaluate_c5(best_h)
    try:
        ev = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if ev is not None:
            if isinstance(ev, (tuple, list)):
                ev = ev[0]
            ev = float(ev)
            if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < final_c:
                final_c = ev
    except Exception:
        pass
    final_c = float(final_c)
    print("FINAL n={} C5={: .7f}".format(best_n, final_c), flush=True)
    return (best_h, final_c, best_n)
```
